# 01-papers

Publisher PDFs and citations: 479 papers. The folders `01-papers`, `02-fulltext`, and `03-reviews` use the same `<theme>/<year>-<short-name>-<ID>/` layout.

[Library overview](../README.md) · [Reading guide](../READING_GUIDE.md)

PDFs and full-text transcriptions are delivered through Git LFS. Run `git lfs pull` after cloning, then open the reading files at the repository-relative paths below. ★ marks the 18 key papers of the [reading guide](../READING_GUIDE.md). Module, Evidence, and Platform are the `survey_module`, `evidence_type`, and `platform_class` values of each review's survey coding ([vocabulary](../06-data/vocabulary.md)). Empty coding cells indicate that survey classification is pending. The Assessment column distinguishes source notes and unassessed files from review cards; supplemental surveys retain their scope status in the catalog.
